# Fox (lntrn-file-manager) bug audit, 2026-10-02

13 finders read the crate line by line; 7 skeptics then re-read the code and tried to refute each finding. Each finding below was checked by one skeptic. "Fix safe" is the skeptic's call that the fix is local and mechanical.

Raw findings: 357. Confirmed: 233. Plausible (defect is in the code, reachability or impact not established): 17. Duplicates folded: 105. Refuted: 2.

Fixed: 247 of 250. Partly fixed: 3. Still open: 0.

How it was fixed: pass 1 (139 findings, one author) was reviewed by 12 readers + 12 skeptics and committed as f4941dd. Batch 2 (the other 118 findings, twelve implementers in three lanes) was reviewed by 10 readers + 6 skeptics; what they confirmed is listed under "Review of batch 2" below. Everything builds without warnings and passes the crate's tests. NONE of batch 2 has been run in the app, against real Firebase, with real sudo or against real hardware.

## Review of the fixes

After the fix pass, 12 reviewers read the diff and 12 skeptics tried to refute what they reported: 35 findings (34 confirmed, 1 plausible, 0 refuted; five of them are the integration reviewer re-finding an area finding). The fixes for these were made by the same author and have not themselves been reviewed.
